चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); इति च इति च = ‘thus and thus, such and such’
वक्तव्याvaktavyāis to be toldgerundive (tavya) of √वच् (adādi, 2P), f. nom. sg.; predicate of सा
साsāshepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; subject of the passive वक्तव्या
गत्वाgatvāhaving goneabsolutive (ktvā) of √गम् (bhvādi, 1P)
वचनात्vacanāton the word, by the commandn. abl. sg. of वचन; ablative of authority
ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; qualifies वचनात्
यथाyathāso that, in such a way thatindeclinable (relative adverb; correlative of तथा)
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
अभ्येतिabhyeticomes (to me)अभि + √इ (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
सम्प्रीत्याsamprītyāwith good will, gladlyf. instr. sg. of सम्प्रीति; instrumental of manner
तथाtathāso, thusindeclinable (correlative of यथा)
कार्यम्kāryam(it) is to be donegerundive (ṇyat) of √कृ, n. nom. sg., impersonal
त्वयाtvayāby yousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, instr. sg.; agent of कार्यम्
लघुlaghuquicklyn. acc. sg. of लघु, used adverbially

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • इति चेति च iti ceti ca → इति + च + इति + च
    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + i → e (च + इति → चेति).
  • वचनान्मम vacanānmama → वचनात् + मम
    consonant sandhi (nasal assimilation) · यरोऽनुनासिकेऽनुनासिको वा (8.4.45)
    Final t before the nasal m becomes n.
  • चाभ्येति cābhyeti → च + अभ्येति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā. Inside the verb, अभि + एति → अभ्येति by yaṇ (6.1.77).
  • कार्यं त्वया kāryaṃ tvayā → कार्यम् + त्वया
    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra.

C. Morphology

  • वक्तव्या vaktavyā — Gerundive of √वच् (वच् + tavya, with c → k): ‘she is to be told’. The person told is the grammatical subject, as with verbs of speaking in the passive.
  • गत्वा gatvā — Absolutive of √गम् (the m drops before -tvā). Its doer is the messenger, who is also the logical agent of वक्तव्या and of कार्यम् (त्वया).
  • अभ्येति abhyeti — Present of √इ ‘go’ (एति) with the prefix अभि ‘towards’: ‘comes to (me)’. Present tense after यथा for a purpose: ‘so that she comes’.
  • कार्यम् kāryam — Gerundive of √कृ with the affix ṇyat and vṛddhi (कार्-य): ‘to be done’, used impersonally with the agent त्वया.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(त्वया) गत्वा मम वचनात् सा ‘इति च इति च’ वक्तव्या; यथा च (सा) सम्प्रीत्या अभ्येति, तथा त्वया लघु कार्यम्।

(tvayā) gatvā mama vacanāt sā ‘iti ca iti ca’ vaktavyā; yathā ca (sā) samprītyā abhyeti, tathā tvayā laghu kāryam |

Śumbha’s instructions in two passive sentences built on gerundives: वक्तव्या (‘she is to be told’) and कार्यम् (‘it is to be done’), the second with a यथा … तथा clause of purpose.

6Translation

LiteralHaving gone, she is to be told on my word such-and-such; and so that she comes with good will, so is it to be done by you, quickly.

Readable‘Go to her and tell her this and this from me; and act quickly, so that she comes to me willingly.’

The gerundives are rendered as imperatives addressed to the messenger, which is their force.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningŚumbha’s instructions to Sugrīva, given without a speaker label: speak to her on my authority, and bring her quickly and willingly. इति च इति च stands for the message itself, which the envoy delivers in full in 5.106–114.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: Śumbha wants her consent (सम्प्रीत्या, ‘gladly’) but only as a means — ‘arrange it so that she comes’. Courtesy put to the service of possession is still possession.

5.104Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

स तत्र गत्वा यत्रास्ते शैलोद्देशेऽतिशोभने।
सा देवी तां ततः प्राह श्लक्ष्णं मधुरया गिरा ॥ १०४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

सः | तत्र | गत्वा | यत्र | आस्ते | शैलोद्देशे | अतिशोभनेसा | देवी | ताम् | ततः | प्राह | श्लक्ष्णम् | मधुरया | गिरा

3IAST

As recitedsa tatra gatvā yatrāste śailoddeśe'tiśobhane |
sā devī tāṃ tataḥ prāha ślakṣṇaṃ madhurayā girā ||

Separatedsaḥ | tatra | gatvā | yatra | āste | śailoddeśe | atiśobhane
sā | devī | tām | tataḥ | prāha | ślakṣṇam | madhurayā | girā

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
सःsaḥhe (Sugrīva)pronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of प्राह
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place; correlative of यत्र)
गत्वाgatvāhaving goneabsolutive (ktvā) of √गम्
यत्रyatrawhereindeclinable (relative adverb of place)
आस्तेāstewas staying√आस् (adādi, 2Ā), laṭ (present, here with past sense), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada
शैलोद्देशेśailoddeśein a region of the mountainm. loc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa)
अतिशोभनेatiśobhanemost beautifulm. loc. sg. of अतिशोभन; qualifies शैलोद्देशे
साsāthatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies देवी
देवीdevīthe Devīf. nom. sg. of देवी; subject of आस्ते (in the kept reading)
ताम्tāmto herpronoun तद्, f. acc. sg.; the person addressed by प्राह
ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
प्राहprāhaspokeप्र + √अह् (defective root ‘to say’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
श्लक्ष्णम्ślakṣṇamgently, smoothlyn. acc. sg. of श्लक्ष्ण, used adverbially
मधुरयाmadhurayāwith sweetf. instr. sg. of मधुर; qualifies गिरा
गिराgirāspeech, voicef. instr. sg. of गिर् (consonant stem); instrumental of means

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • स तत्र sa tatra → सः + तत्र
    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)
    The visarga of सः is dropped before a consonant.
  • यत्रास्ते yatrāste → यत्र + आस्ते
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • शैलोद्देशेऽतिशोभने śailoddeśe'tiśobhane → शैलोद्देशे + अतिशोभने
    vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)
    -e + a- → -e’- (avagraha). Inside the compound, शैल + उद्देश → शैलोद्देश by guṇa (6.1.87).
  • तां ततः प्राह tāṃ tataḥ prāha → ताम् + ततः + प्राह
    anusvāra sandhi; visarga before p · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)
    Final m before a consonant becomes anusvāra; before the unvoiced p the visarga of ततः stays visarga.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शैलोद्देशे śailoddeśe — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: शैल + उद्देश · Vigraha: शैलस्य उद्देशः, तस्मिन् śailasya uddeśaḥ, tasmin
    Literally “in a region of the mountain”; in the verse: on a spot of the Himālaya
  • अतिशोभने atiśobhane — prādi tatpuruṣa
    Members: अति + शोभन · Vigraha: अतिशयेन शोभनः, तस्मिन् atiśayena śobhanaḥ, tasmin
    Literally “exceedingly beautiful”; in the verse: most lovely

C. Morphology

  • प्राह prāha — आह is the perfect of the defective root √अह् ‘say’, used with present or past sense; with प्र: प्राह ‘spoke, said’. It takes the person addressed in the accusative (ताम्).
  • गिरा girā — Instrumental singular of the r-stem गिर् ‘voice, speech’ (nom. गीः).
  • आस्ते āste — Present tense in a past narrative (‘where she was staying’), as often in a relative clause of place.

D. Anvaya — prose order

सः, यत्र अतिशोभने शैलोद्देशे सा देवी आस्ते, तत्र गत्वा, ततः तां मधुरया गिरा श्लक्ष्णं प्राह।

saḥ, yatra atiśobhane śailoddeśe sā devī āste, tatra gatvā, tataḥ tāṃ madhurayā girā ślakṣṇaṃ prāha |

Main clause सः … प्राह; an absolutive (तत्र गत्वा) with a relative clause of place (यत्र … आस्ते) whose subject सा देवी stands in the second line.

6Translation

LiteralHe, having gone there where that Devī was staying, on a most beautiful region of the mountain, then spoke to her gently with sweet speech.

ReadableSugrīva went to the lovely mountain slope where the Goddess was staying, and addressed her gently, in honeyed words.

The kept reading places सा देवी, the subject of the relative clause, in the second line; the translation brings it back into that clause.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe envoy reaches the Goddess on the mountain and speaks to her softly. His gentleness belongs to the instructions he was given (5.103: bring her ‘with good will’).

Traditional interpretationText: GRETIL and Freund read तां च देवीं ततः प्राह ‘and then he spoke to that Devī’, which makes the second line a simple object clause. The Gita Press reading सा देवी तां ततः प्राह is kept here, with सा देवी as the subject of यत्रास्ते and तां resuming her as the person addressed (corrections.json, variants: ‘Textual Verification Required’).

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: Variant reading: the Gita Press has सा देवी तां ततः प्राह; GRETIL and Freund have तां च देवीं ततः प्राह. The kept reading is construed with सा देवी as the displaced subject of यत्र आस्ते; it is grammatical but unusual in order, and the other reading is smoother. Status in corrections.json: Textual Verification Required.

उवाचuvācasaid√वच् (adādi, 2P), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, kartari prayoga

  • दूत उवाच dūta uvāca → दूतः + उवाच
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)
    -aḥ before a vowel other than a: the visarga becomes y, the y is dropped, and the two vowels stand apart (दूत | उवाच), as in मार्कण्डेय उवाच (1.1).

ReadableThe messenger said:

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningSugrīva, Śumbha’s envoy, begins his formal address to the Goddess on the mountain. His speech runs to 5.114.

5.106Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

देवि दैत्येश्वरः शुम्भस्त्रैलोक्ये परमेश्वरः।
दूतोऽहं प्रेषितस्तेन त्वत्सकाशमिहागतः ॥ १०६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

देवि | दैत्येश्वरः | शुम्भः | त्रैलोक्ये | परमेश्वरःदूतः | अहम् | प्रेषितः | तेन | त्वत्सकाशम् | इह | आगतः

3IAST

As reciteddevi daityeśvaraḥ śumbhastrailokye parameśvaraḥ |
dūto'haṃ preṣitastena tvatsakāśamihāgataḥ ||

Separateddevi | daityeśvaraḥ | śumbhaḥ | trailokye | parameśvaraḥ
dūtaḥ | aham | preṣitaḥ | tena | tvatsakāśam | iha | āgataḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
देविdeviO Devīf. voc. sg. of देवी
दैत्येश्वरःdaityeśvaraḥlord of the daityasm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to शुम्भः
शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject (with अस्ति understood)
त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (see 5.93)
परमेश्वरःparameśvaraḥthe supreme lordm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate
दूतःdūtaḥa messengerm. nom. sg. of दूत; predicate of अहम्
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.
प्रेषितःpreṣitaḥsentpast participle (kta) of the causative of प्र + √इष्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies अहम्
तेनtenaby himpronoun तद्, m. instr. sg.; agent of प्रेषितः
त्वत्सकाशम्tvatsakāśaminto your presencem. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); goal of आगतः
इहihahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
आगतःāgataḥhave comepast participle (kta) of आ + √गम्, m. nom. sg.; qualifies अहम्

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • शुम्भस्त्रैलोक्ये śumbhastrailokye → शुम्भः + त्रैलोक्ये
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s.
  • दूतोऽहं dūto'haṃ → दूतः + अहम्
    visarga sandhi, then vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · अतो रोरप्लुतादप्लुते (6.1.113), आद्गुणः (6.1.87), एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)
    -aḥ before a becomes -o and the following a is elided (avagraha); अहम् then takes anusvāra before p (8.3.23).
  • प्रेषितस्तेन preṣitastena → प्रेषितः + तेन
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s.
  • त्वत्सकाशमिहागतः tvatsakāśamihāgataḥ → त्वत्सकाशम् + इह + आगतः
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    इह + आगतः: a + ā → ā; the m of त्वत्सकाशम् stays m before the vowel and is written joined.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • दैत्येश्वरः daityeśvaraḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: दैत्य + ईश्वर · Vigraha: दैत्यानाम् ईश्वरः daityānām īśvaraḥ
    Literally “lord of the daityas”; in the verse: Śumbha; a + ī → e by guṇa (6.1.87)
  • परमेश्वरः parameśvaraḥ — karmadhāraya
    Members: परम + ईश्वर · Vigraha: परमः ईश्वरः paramaḥ īśvaraḥ
    Literally “supreme lord”; in the verse: the highest ruler — Śumbha’s claim for himself
  • त्वत्सकाशम् tvatsakāśam — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (त्वद् is the compound stem of युष्मद् in the singular)
    Members: त्वद् + सकाश · Vigraha: तव सकाशम् tava sakāśam
    Literally “your vicinity”; in the verse: into your presence

C. Morphology

  • प्रेषितः preṣitaḥ — Past participle of the causative प्रेषय- (प्र + √इष्): the causative -aya- is dropped before -ita. Compare प्रेषयामास (5.102).
  • आगतः āgataḥ — Past participle of आ + √गम् with active sense for a verb of motion: ‘(I) have come’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

देवि, दैत्येश्वरः शुम्भः त्रैलोक्ये परमेश्वरः (अस्ति)। तेन प्रेषितः दूतः अहं त्वत्सकाशम् इह आगतः (अस्मि)।

devi, daityeśvaraḥ śumbhaḥ trailokye parameśvaraḥ (asti) | tena preṣitaḥ dūtaḥ ahaṃ tvatsakāśam iha āgataḥ (asmi) |

Two nominal sentences with the copula understood: Śumbha’s status, then the messenger’s own (अहम् with दूतः, प्रेषितः, आगतः).

6Translation

LiteralO Devī, Śumbha, lord of the daityas, is the supreme lord in the three worlds. I, a messenger sent by him, have come here into your presence.

Readable‘Goddess, Śumbha, lord of the demons, is supreme sovereign of the three worlds. I am his envoy, sent by him, and have come here before you.’

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningSugrīva opens with protocol: he names his master’s title and his own office. Śumbha is called परमेश्वरः, ‘supreme lord’, in the three worlds.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the title is ironic in context. The gods’ hymn has just praised the Goddess as the power within all beings, and the one the envoy addresses as ‘Devī’ is the sovereign he does not recognise.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāLalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) names the Goddess परमेश्वरी (name 396), the feminine of the title Śumbha claims here. The comparison is ours; the Devī Māhātmya itself makes the irony through its story.

5.107Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

अव्याहताज्ञः सर्वासु यः सदा देवयोनिषु।
निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः स यदाह शृणुष्व तत् ॥ १०७ ॥

अव्याहताज्ञः | सर्वासु | यः | सदा | देवयोनिषुनिर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः | सः | यत् | आह | शृणुष्व | तत्

nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ sa yadāha śṛṇuṣva tat ||

nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ | saḥ | yat | āha | śṛṇuṣva | tat

अव्याहताज्ञःavyāhatājñaḥwhose command is unobstructedm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of यः

यःyaḥwhorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; refers to Śumbha; its correlative is सः
सदाsadāalwaysindeclinable (adverb of time)

शृणुष्वśṛṇuṣvahear, listen to√श्रु (svādi 5 ‘to hear’, stem शृणु-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada (an epic use; classical Sanskrit has the parasmaipada शृणु)
तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. acc. sg.; correlative of यत्; object of शृणुष्व

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ → अ + वि + आहत + आज्ञः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (yaṇ and savarṇa-dīrgha) · इको यणचि (6.1.77); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    i before ā becomes y (वि + आहत → व्याहत); a + ā → ā (व्याहत + आज्ञा → व्याहताज्ञ).
  • निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ → निर्जित + अखिल + दैत्य + अरिः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    Twice a + a → ā: निर्जिताखिल, दैत्यारि.
  • स यदाह sa yadāha → सः + यत् + आह
    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः); consonant sandhi (voicing) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132); झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    The visarga of सः is dropped before the consonant y; the final t of यत् becomes d before the vowel of आह.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ — bahuvrīhi (its first member अव्याहत is a nañ tatpuruṣa: न व्याहता)
    Members: अव्याहत + आज्ञा · Vigraha: अव्याहता आज्ञा यस्य सः avyāhatā ājñā yasya saḥ
    Literally “one whose command is not struck down”; in the verse: a ruler whose orders no one resists
  • देवयोनिषु devayoniṣu — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: देव + योनि · Vigraha: देवानां योनयः, तासु devānāṃ yonayaḥ, tāsu
    Literally “among the kinds (births) of the gods”; in the verse: among all the orders of superhuman beings
    Alternative: देवयोनि is often a bahuvrīhi, देवः योनिः येषां ते ‘beings of divine origin’ (masculine). Here the feminine सर्वासु shows that योनि ‘kind, birth’ (f.) is the head, so the tatpuruṣa fits.
  • निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ — bahuvrīhi (inside it दैत्यारि is a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, दैत्यानाम् अरिः)
    Members: निर्जित + अखिल + दैत्यारि · Vigraha: निर्जिताः अखिलाः दैत्यारयः येन सः nirjitāḥ akhilāḥ daityārayaḥ yena saḥ
    Literally “one by whom all the Daitya-enemies have been conquered”; in the verse: the conqueror of all the gods
    Alternative: दैत्यारि ‘enemy of the Daityas’ can name Viṣṇu alone; with अखिल ‘all’ the whole body of gods is meant.

C. Morphology

  • आह āha — √ब्रू has in the present the substitute आह with perfect-type endings (आह, आहतुः, आहुः); Pāṇini allows it optionally beside ब्रवीति. The sense is present: ‘(he) says’.
  • शृणुष्व śṛṇuṣva — Imperative ātmanepada of √श्रु. The root is parasmaipada in Pāṇini; the ātmanepada imperative is common in the epics and Purāṇas, with no difference of sense.
  • अव्याहताज्ञः avyāhatājñaḥ — The feminine आज्ञा is shortened to आज्ञ as the last member of a bahuvrīhi that qualifies a masculine: ‘he whose command …’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

यः सदा सर्वासु देवयोनिषु अव्याहताज्ञः, निर्जिताखिलदैत्यारिः सः (शुम्भः) यत् आह, तत् शृणुष्व।

yaḥ sadā sarvāsu devayoniṣu avyāhatājñaḥ, nirjitākhiladaityāriḥ saḥ (śumbhaḥ) yat āha, tat śṛṇuṣva |

A relative clause (यः … अव्याहताज्ञः) and a second epithet describe the subject सः; inside the main clause a second relative pair (यत् … तत्) makes the object of the imperative शृणुष्व.

6Translation

LiteralHe who always (has) unobstructed command among all the divine orders, (and) who has conquered all the enemies of the Daityas — hear that which he says.

ReadableAmong all the orders of heavenly beings his command is never resisted, and he has vanquished every foe of the Daityas. Hear what he says.

The relative clause is turned into two plain statements.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe messenger Sugrīva, sent by Śumbha (5.102–104), finishes introducing his master (5.106) before delivering the message. The two titles summarise the opening of the chapter: the brothers have taken the gods’ offices and driven them out (5.2–5).

Traditional interpretationदेवयोनि is the usual name for the superhuman orders below the gods proper; the lexicon Amarakośa lists ten (vidyādharas, apsarases, yakṣas, rakṣases, gandharvas, kinnaras, piśācas, guhyakas, siddhas and bhūtas). The claim is that every such order obeys Śumbha.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the message is preceded by credentials. Power announces itself before it asks, so that the asking sounds like a favour.

5.108Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

मम त्रैलोक्यमखिलं मम देवा वशानुगाः।
यज्ञभागानहं सर्वानुपाश्नामि पृथक् पृथक् ॥ १०८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

मम | त्रैलोक्यम् | अखिलम् | मम | देवाः | वशानुगाःयज्ञभागान् | अहम् | सर्वान् | उपाश्नामि | पृथक् | पृथक्

3IAST

As recitedmama trailokyamakhilaṃ mama devā vaśānugāḥ |
yajñabhāgānahaṃ sarvānupāśnāmi pṛthak pṛthak ||

Separatedmama | trailokyam | akhilam | mama | devāḥ | vaśānugāḥ
yajñabhāgān | aham | sarvān | upāśnāmi | pṛthak | pṛthak

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ममmamamine, of mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; predicative possessive (‘is mine’)
त्रैलोक्यम्trailokyamthe three worldsn. nom. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (taddhita from त्रिलोकी); subject (verb ‘is’ understood)
अखिलम्akhilamentire, wholeadjective अखिल, n. nom. sg.; qualifies त्रैलोक्यम्
ममmamamy, to mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; depends on वशानुगाः
देवाःdevāḥthe godsm. nom. pl. of देव; subject
वशानुगाःvaśānugāḥobedient, following (my) willm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of देवाः
यज्ञभागान्yajñabhāgānthe shares of the sacrificem. acc. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); object of उपाश्नामि
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
सर्वान्sarvānallpronominal adjective सर्व, m. acc. pl.; qualifies यज्ञभागान्
उपाश्नामिupāśnāmiI enjoy, I consumeउप + √अश् (kryādi 9P ‘to eat’, stem अश्ना-), laṭ (present), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
पृथक्pṛthakseparatelyindeclinable (adverb)
पृथक्pṛthak(each) separatelyindeclinable; repeated (āmreḍita) to express distribution: ‘each one apart’

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • त्रैलोक्यमखिलं मम trailokyamakhilaṃ mama → त्रैलोक्यम् + अखिलम् + मम
    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m stays m before the vowel of अखिलम् and is simply written with it; before the consonant of मम it becomes anusvāra.
  • देवा वशानुगाः devā vaśānugāḥ → देवाः + वशानुगाः
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)
    -āḥ before a voiced sound: the visarga becomes y and the y is dropped, leaving -ā.
  • यज्ञभागानहं yajñabhāgānahaṃ → यज्ञभागान् + अहम्
    no change (final n before a vowel) · ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) does not apply
    The n is not doubled, because the vowel before it (ā) is long; it is simply joined to अहम्. The same in सर्वानुपाश्नामि.
  • वशानुगाः vaśānugāḥ → वश + अनुगाः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वशानुगाः vaśānugāḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa (अनुग = अनु + √गम् + ḍa)
    Members: वश + अनुग · Vigraha: वशम् अनुगच्छन्ति इति वशानुगाः vaśam anugacchanti iti vaśānugāḥ
    Literally “those who follow the will”; in the verse: subject to my command
  • यज्ञभागान् yajñabhāgān — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: यज्ञ + भाग · Vigraha: यज्ञस्य भागाः, तान् yajñasya bhāgāḥ, tān
    Literally “the portions of the sacrifice”; in the verse: the offerings due to each god at the rite
  • त्रैलोक्यम् trailokyam — samāhāra dvigu (त्रिलोकी), made into a noun with the taddhita ṣyañ that adds no new sense
    Members: त्रि + लोक (+ taddhita ṣyañ) · Vigraha: त्रयाणां लोकानां समाहारः त्रिलोकी, सा एव त्रैलोक्यम् trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ samāhāraḥ trilokī, sā eva trailokyam
    Literally “the group of three worlds”; in the verse: the whole universe: earth, the middle region and heaven

C. Morphology

  • उपाश्नामि upāśnāmi — √अश् ‘to eat’ belongs to the ninth class: अश् + ना → अश्नामि. With उप the prefix and the root vowel merge (उप + अश्नामि → उपाश्नामि). The verb of eating is chosen for the sacrificial shares, which the gods ‘eat’.
  • पृथक् pṛthak — The repeated adverb (पृथक् पृथक्) expresses distribution, ‘one by one, each share apart’ — every god’s portion now goes to Śumbha.

D. Anvaya — prose order

अखिलं त्रैलोक्यं मम (अस्ति); देवाः मम वशानुगाः (सन्ति); अहं सर्वान् यज्ञभागान् पृथक् पृथक् उपाश्नामि।

akhilaṃ trailokyaṃ mama (asti); devāḥ mama vaśānugāḥ (santi); ahaṃ sarvān yajñabhāgān pṛthak pṛthak upāśnāmi |

Three clauses, the first two nominal (the verb ‘is / are’ understood), the third with the finite verb उपाश्नामि. From here to 5.114 the messenger speaks in Śumbha’s first person.

6Translation

LiteralMine is the entire triple world; mine are the gods, following my will. All the shares of the sacrifice I enjoy, each separately.

Readable“The three worlds are mine entire, and the gods obey my will. Every share of the sacrifice, one by one, is mine to enjoy.

The opening quotation mark shows that the message is quoted in Śumbha’s own words.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe message proper begins, in Śumbha’s voice. Its claims repeat the narrator’s account at the head of the chapter: the three worlds and the sacrificial shares were taken from Indra (5.2), and the gods were driven out (5.4–5).

Traditional interpretationThe sacrificial share (यज्ञभाग) is the gods’ sustenance and the sign of their office. In the Purāṇic view, a demon who eats the shares has overturned the order of the world, not merely won a war; the Devī’s own message in 8.26 demands that the gods again be ‘eaters of oblations’ (हविर्भुजः), and 12.34 records that they enjoyed their shares once more.

5.109Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

त्रैलोक्ये वररत्नानि मम वश्यान्यशेषतः।
तथैव गजरत्नं च हृत्वा देवेन्द्रवाहनम् ॥ १०९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

त्रैलोक्ये | वररत्नानि | मम | वश्यानि | अशेषतःतथा | एव | गजरत्नम् | च | हृत्वा | देवेन्द्रवाहनम्

3IAST

As recitedtrailokye vararatnāni mama vaśyānyaśeṣataḥ |
tathaiva gajaratnaṃ ca hṛtvā devendravāhanam ||

Separatedtrailokye | vararatnāni | mama | vaśyāni | aśeṣataḥ
tathā | eva | gajaratnam | ca | hṛtvā | devendravāhanam

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य
वररत्नानिvararatnānithe choicest jewelsn. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); subject
ममmamato mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with वश्यानि ‘subject to me’
वश्यानिvaśyānisubject, under controladjective वश्य (वश + taddhita yat), n. nom. pl.; predicate of वररत्नानि
अशेषतःaśeṣataḥwithout exception, entirelyindeclinable (अशेष + tasil, adverb)
तथाtathāso, likewiseindeclinable (adverb of manner)
एवevajust, indeedindeclinable (emphatic particle); तथा एव ‘in just the same way’
गजरत्नम्gajaratnamthe jewel of elephantsn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of हृत्वा
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
हृत्वाhṛtvāhaving seizedabsolutive (ktvā) of √हृ (bhvādi 1U ‘to take, carry off’)
देवेन्द्रवाहनम्devendravāhanamthe mount of the lord of the gods (Indra)n. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); in apposition to गजरत्नम्

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • वश्यान्यशेषतः vaśyānyaśeṣataḥ → वश्यानि + अशेषतः
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    Final i before a different vowel becomes y.
  • तथैव tathaiva → तथा + एव
    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    ā + e → ai.
  • देवेन्द्रवाहनम् devendravāhanam → देव + इन्द्र + वाहनम्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + i → e.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • वररत्नानि vararatnāni — karmadhāraya
    Members: वर + रत्न · Vigraha: वराणि च तानि रत्नानि च varāṇi ca tāni ratnāni ca
    Literally “excellent jewels”; in the verse: the finest treasures of every kind
    Alternative: Also read as a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, रत्नानां वराणि ‘the best of jewels’; the sense is the same.
  • गजरत्नम् gajaratnam — saptamī tatpuruṣa (of selection), as in 5.94
    Members: गज + रत्न · Vigraha: गजेषु रत्नम् gajeṣu ratnam
    Literally “jewel among elephants”; in the verse: the finest of elephants, Airāvata (named in 5.94)
    Alternative: Or ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, गजानां रत्नम्, with the same sense.
  • देवेन्द्रवाहनम् devendravāhanam — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, whose first member is also a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: देवेन्द्र + वाहन · Vigraha: देवेन्द्रस्य वाहनम् (देवेन्द्रः = देवानाम् इन्द्रः) devendrasya vāhanam (devendraḥ = devānām indraḥ)
    Literally “the vehicle of the chief of the gods”; in the verse: Indra’s mount

C. Morphology

  • हृत्वा hṛtvā — Absolutive of √हृ (no prefix, so -त्वा, not -य). It has no finite verb of its own in the verse: the sense ‘(is mine), having been seized by me’ is carried over from मम वश्यानि. A loose construction typical of reported speech.
  • वश्यानि vaśyāni — वश ‘control’ + the taddhita -य: ‘what is in (one’s) control’. With the genitive मम: ‘subject to me’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

त्रैलोक्ये (यानि) वररत्नानि (तानि) अशेषतः मम वश्यानि (सन्ति); तथा एव देवेन्द्रवाहनं गजरत्नं च (मया) हृत्वा (मम वश्यम्)।

trailokye (yāni) vararatnāni (tāni) aśeṣataḥ mama vaśyāni (santi); tathā eva devendravāhanaṃ gajaratnaṃ ca (mayā) hṛtvā (mama vaśyam) |

The first line is a nominal sentence (verb understood). The second hangs on the absolutive हृत्वा and borrows its predicate from the first: the elephant too is ‘subject to me’.

6Translation

LiteralIn the three worlds the choice jewels are subject to me without exception; and likewise the jewel of elephants, the mount of the lord of the gods, having seized (it) —

ReadableEvery treasure of worth in the three worlds belongs to me, without exception — so too the king of elephants, Indra’s own mount, which I have taken.

The absolutive हृत्वा is rendered as a relative clause ‘which I have taken’, completing the loose syntax.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningŚumbha’s message repeats what Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa told him (5.93–94): the jewels of the three worlds are in his palace, and Indra’s elephant Airāvata among them. The messenger turns their report into a boast.

Traditional interpretationरत्न ‘jewel’ in this passage means the best of any kind — elephant, horse, tree, woman — not only gems. The list prepares the inference of 5.112: if all jewels are his, the ‘jewel of women’ must be his too.

5.110Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतमश्वरत्नं ममामरैः।
उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञं तत्प्रणिपत्य समर्पितम् ॥ ११० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् | अश्वरत्नम् | मम | अमरैःउच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् | तत् | प्रणिपत्य | समर्पितम्

3IAST

As recitedkṣīrodamathanodbhūtamaśvaratnaṃ mamāmaraiḥ |
uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñaṃ tatpraṇipatya samarpitam ||

Separatedkṣīrodamathanodbhūtam | aśvaratnam | mama | amaraiḥ
uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam | tat | praṇipatya | samarpitam

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम्kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamthat arose from the churning of the Milk Oceann. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अश्वरत्नम्
अश्वरत्नम्aśvaratnamthe jewel of horsesn. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of the passive समर्पितम्
ममmamato mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg., used for the dative (recipient) with समर्पितम्
अमरैःamaraiḥby the immortals (the gods)m. instr. pl. of अमर; agent of समर्पितम्
उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम्uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñamnamed Uccaiḥśravasn. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies अश्वरत्नम्

प्रणिपत्यpraṇipatyahaving bowed downabsolutive (lyap) of प्र + नि + √पत् (bhvādi 1P ‘to fall’); its subject is the agent अमरैः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतमश्वरत्नं kṣīrodamathanodbhūtamaśvaratnaṃ → क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् + अश्वरत्नम्
    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m is joined to the vowel of अश्वरत्नम्; the m of अश्वरत्नम् becomes anusvāra before मम.
  • ममामरैः mamāmaraiḥ → मम + अमरैः
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूत kṣīrodamathanodbhūta → क्षीरोद + मथन + उद्भूत
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + u → o (मथनोद्भूत).
  • तत्प्रणिपत्य tatpraṇipatya → तत् + प्रणिपत्य
    no change · खरि च (8.4.55)
    Final t stays unvoiced before the unvoiced p; the words are only written together.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् kṣīrodamathanodbhūtam — pañcamī tatpuruṣa containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (क्षीरोदमथन)
    Members: क्षीरोदमथन + उद्भूत · Vigraha: क्षीरोदस्य मथनम् क्षीरोदमथनम्; क्षीरोदमथनात् उद्भूतम् kṣīrodasya mathanam kṣīrodamathanam; kṣīrodamathanāt udbhūtam
    Literally “arisen from the churning of the milk-water (ocean)”; in the verse: one of the treasures that came up when gods and asuras churned the ocean
    Alternative: क्षीरोद itself is a bahuvrīhi, क्षीरम् उदकं यस्य सः ‘that whose water is milk’, with उदक shortened to उद.
  • अश्वरत्नम् aśvaratnam — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (of selection)
    Members: अश्व + रत्न · Vigraha: अश्वानां रत्नम् aśvānāṃ ratnam
    Literally “the jewel of horses”; in the verse: the finest of horses
  • उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam — bahuvrīhi
    Members: उच्चैःश्रवस + संज्ञा · Vigraha: उच्चैःश्रवसः संज्ञा यस्य तत् uccaiḥśravasaḥ saṃjñā yasya tat
    Literally “having the name Uccaiḥśravas”; in the verse: the horse called Uccaiḥśravas
    Alternative: The name उच्चैःश्रवस् is itself a bahuvrīhi, उच्चैः श्रवसी यस्य सः ‘whose ears are high’ (or ‘whose neigh is loud’).

C. Morphology

  • समर्पितम् samarpitam — Past passive participle of the causative of सम् + √ऋ (‘cause to go to’ = ‘hand over’): सम्-अर्पित. With the instrumental अमरैः it is a passive sentence: ‘(it) was surrendered by the gods’.
  • प्रणिपत्य praṇipatya — Absolutive with prefixes, so -य (lyap). In a passive sentence its subject is the agent in the instrumental: the gods bowed, then handed over the horse.
  • उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञम् uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñam — The horse’s name is the s-stem उच्चैःश्रवस्; in this compound it appears as उच्चैःश्रवस-, as if an a-stem, a by-form found in epic and Purāṇic usage. The visarga of उच्चैः is kept inside the compound, as printed.

D. Anvaya — prose order

क्षीरोदमथनोद्भूतम् उच्चैःश्रवससंज्ञं तत् अश्वरत्नम् अमरैः प्रणिपत्य मम समर्पितम्।

kṣīrodamathanodbhūtam uccaiḥśravasasaṃjñaṃ tat aśvaratnam amaraiḥ praṇipatya mama samarpitam |

A passive sentence: subject अश्वरत्नम् with three qualifiers, agent अमरैः, recipient मम, predicate समर्पितम्; the absolutive प्रणिपत्य belongs to the agent.

6Translation

LiteralThat jewel of horses called Uccaiḥśravas, which arose from the churning of the Milk Ocean, was handed over to me by the immortals, having bowed down.

ReadableUccaiḥśravas, the finest of horses, born from the churning of the Ocean of Milk, the gods themselves surrendered to me with bowed heads.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe second of Indra’s great treasures: the horse Uccaiḥśravas, already listed by Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa (5.94). Śumbha adds a detail they did not give — the gods bowed as they handed it over.

Traditional interpretationUccaiḥśravas is one of the treasures that rose from the ocean when the gods and asuras churned it, and belongs to Indra in the Purāṇic accounts. A treasure won by the gods from that churning is now given up to an asura: the boast measures how complete his victory is.

5.111Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यानि चान्यानि देवेषु गन्धर्वेषूरगेषु च।
रत्नभूतानि भूतानि तानि मय्येव शोभने ॥ १११ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यानि | च | अन्यानि | देवेषु | गन्धर्वेषु | उरगेषु | चरत्नभूतानि | भूतानि | तानि | मयि | एव | शोभने

3IAST

As recitedyāni cānyāni deveṣu gandharveṣūrageṣu ca |
ratnabhūtāni bhūtāni tāni mayyeva śobhane ||

Separatedyāni | ca | anyāni | deveṣu | gandharveṣu | urageṣu | ca
ratnabhūtāni | bhūtāni | tāni | mayi | eva | śobhane

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यानिyāniwhich, whateverrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. pl.; qualifies भूतानि; correlative तानि
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); joins this verse to the list before it
अन्यानिanyāniotherpronominal adjective अन्य, n. nom. pl.; qualifies भूतानि
देवेषुdeveṣuamong the godsm. loc. pl. of देव
गन्धर्वेषुgandharveṣuamong the Gandharvasm. loc. pl. of गन्धर्व
उरगेषुurageṣuamong the serpents (Nāgas)m. loc. pl. of उरग (‘going on the breast’: उरस् + √गम् + ḍa)
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction); closes the list
रत्नभूतानिratnabhūtānithat are jewels, of jewel rankn. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies भूतानि
भूतानिbhūtānithings, beingsn. nom. pl. of भूत (noun: ‘what has come to be’); subject of the relative clause
तानिtānithey, thosepronoun तद्, n. nom. pl.; correlative of यानि; subject of the main clause
मयिmayiin me, with mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, loc. sg.
एवevaalone, onlyindeclinable (restrictive particle)
शोभनेśobhaneO beautiful onef. voc. sg. of शोभना (√शुभ् + lyuṭ, ‘shining, lovely’); addresses the Devī

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • चान्यानि cānyāni → च + अन्यानि
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • गन्धर्वेषूरगेषु gandharveṣūrageṣu → गन्धर्वेषु + उरगेषु
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    u + u → ū.
  • मय्येव mayyeva → मयि + एव
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    The final i of मयि becomes y before e, so the y of the stem and the new y stand together (मय्येव).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • रत्नभूतानि ratnabhūtāni — karmadhāraya (भूत ‘being, become’ as final member)
    Members: रत्न + भूत · Vigraha: रत्नानि च तानि भूतानि च ratnāni ca tāni bhūtāni ca
    Literally “that have become jewels, that are jewels”; in the verse: treasures of the first rank among their kind

C. Morphology

  • भूतानि bhūtāni — The same word stands twice in a row with two functions: in रत्नभूतानि ‘being jewels’ it is the participle of √भू; alone, भूतानि is the noun ‘things, beings’. The play (रत्नभूतानि भूतानि) is deliberate: ‘things that are jewels’.
  • शोभने śobhane — Vocative of the feminine शोभना: the -ā stem has -e in the vocative singular. The flattering address prepares the proposal of 5.112.

D. Anvaya — prose order

शोभने, देवेषु गन्धर्वेषु उरगेषु च यानि च अन्यानि रत्नभूतानि भूतानि (सन्ति), तानि मयि एव (सन्ति)।

śobhane, deveṣu gandharveṣu urageṣu ca yāni ca anyāni ratnabhūtāni bhūtāni (santi), tāni mayi eva (santi) |

A relative clause (यानि … भूतानि) names the subject; the main clause तानि मयि एव is nominal, with ‘are’ understood.

6Translation

LiteralAnd whatever other things that are jewels (there are) among the gods, the Gandharvas and the serpents — they (are) in me alone, O beautiful one.

ReadableAnd whatever else ranks as a jewel among gods, Gandharvas and serpents — all of it, lovely one, rests with me alone.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe list of treasures closes with a general claim: every ‘jewel’ of every race is now in Śumbha’s possession. The vocative शोभने turns the boast towards the Devī.

Traditional interpretationThe Devī is addressed first as ‘beautiful’; in the next verse she is placed among the jewels. The argument is the one Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa made (5.100): all jewels are his, so why not this one?

5.112Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

स्त्रीरत्नभूतां त्वां देवि लोके मन्यामहे वयम्।
सा त्वमस्मानुपागच्छ यतो रत्नभुजो वयम् ॥ ११२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् | त्वाम् | देवि | लोके | मन्यामहे | वयम्सा | त्वम् | अस्मान् | उपागच्छ | यतः | रत्नभुजः | वयम्

3IAST

As recitedstrīratnabhūtāṃ tvāṃ devi loke manyāmahe vayam |
sā tvamasmānupāgaccha yato ratnabhujo vayam ||

Separatedstrīratnabhūtām | tvām | devi | loke | manyāmahe | vayam
sā | tvam | asmān | upāgaccha | yataḥ | ratnabhujaḥ | vayam

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
स्त्रीरत्नभूताम्strīratnabhūtāmbeing the jewel among womenf. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate accusative with मन्यामहे
त्वाम्tvāmyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, acc. sg.; object of मन्यामहे
देविdeviO Devī, O ladyf. voc. sg. of देवी
लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
मन्यामहेmanyāmahewe consider, we regard√मन् (divādi 4Ā ‘to think’), laṭ (present), uttama-puruṣa bahuvacana, ātmanepada
वयम्vayamwefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. pl.; subject (the royal plural)
साsāsuch (as you are), that onepronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; resumes the description: ‘you, being such’
त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of उपागच्छ
अस्मान्asmānto usfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. pl.; object of उपागच्छ (goal of motion)
उपागच्छupāgacchacome to, approachउप + आ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
यतःyataḥsince, becauseindeclinable (यद् + tasil; causal conjunction)
रत्नभुजःratnabhujaḥenjoyers of jewelsm. nom. pl. of रत्नभुज्; compound (see Samāsa); predicate of वयम्
वयम्vayamwefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. pl.; subject

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • त्वमस्मानुपागच्छ tvamasmānupāgaccha → त्वम् + अस्मान् + उपागच्छ
    no change before vowels · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) and ङमो ह्रस्वादचि ङमुण्नित्यम् (8.3.32) do not apply
    Final m and final n (after the long ā of अस्मान्) are simply joined to the following vowel.
  • उपागच्छ upāgaccha → उप + आ + गच्छ
    vowel sandhi between prefixes (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā.
  • यतो रत्नभुजो वयम् yato ratnabhujo vayam → यतः + रत्नभुजः + वयम्
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before a voiced consonant (r, v) becomes -o, twice.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् strīratnabhūtām — karmadhāraya with भूत ‘being’, containing a saptamī tatpuruṣa (स्त्रीरत्न, of selection)
    Members: स्त्रीरत्न + भूत · Vigraha: स्त्रीषु रत्नम् स्त्रीरत्नम्; स्त्रीरत्नं भूता, ताम् strīṣu ratnam strīratnam; strīratnaṃ bhūtā, tām
    Literally “who has become the jewel among women”; in the verse: the finest of all women
    Alternative: स्त्रीरत्न may also be read as a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa, स्त्रीणां रत्नम्; the sense is the same.
  • रत्नभुजः ratnabhujaḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa (√भुज् + kvip)
    Members: रत्न + भुज् · Vigraha: रत्नानि भुञ्जते इति रत्नभुजः ratnāni bhuñjate iti ratnabhujaḥ
    Literally “jewel-enjoyers”; in the verse: those to whom every treasure belongs by right

C. Morphology

  • मन्यामहे manyāmahe — Present ātmanepada of √मन्, fourth class (मन्य-): ‘we think, regard’. With two accusatives: त्वाम् (object) and स्त्रीरत्नभूताम् (what she is regarded as).
  • रत्नभुजः ratnabhujaḥ — Nominative plural of the root-noun रत्नभुज् (j-final): the bare root with zero affix (kvip) as the final member of an upapada compound, like ‘-bhuj’ in भूभुज् ‘enjoyer of the earth, king’.
  • सा sā — The pronoun सा resumes the predicate of the first line: ‘you, being such (a jewel)’. सा त्वम् is a common Purāṇic opening for a request.

D. Anvaya — prose order

देवि, वयं लोके त्वां स्त्रीरत्नभूतां मन्यामहे। यतः वयं रत्नभुजः, (अतः) सा त्वम् अस्मान् उपागच्छ।

devi, vayaṃ loke tvāṃ strīratnabhūtāṃ manyāmahe | yataḥ vayaṃ ratnabhujaḥ, (ataḥ) sā tvam asmān upāgaccha |

The first sentence has a double accusative (त्वाम् … स्त्रीरत्नभूताम्). The second is an imperative with a causal clause (यतः …) giving the reason.

6Translation

LiteralWe regard you, O Devī, as being the jewel among women in the world. Such as you are, come to us, since we are the enjoyers of jewels.

ReadableWe hold you, O Devī, to be the jewel among the women of the world. Come to us, then, for jewels are ours to enjoy.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe argument of the message is now explicit: every jewel belongs to Śumbha, the Devī is the jewel among women, therefore she must come to him. Śumbha speaks of himself in the plural (वयम्), as a king.

Traditional interpretationThe word रत्न has carried the whole speech from 5.109. The tradition reads the irony of the scene: the one he names a ‘jewel’ to be owned is the power by whom, in the narrator’s words of 5.116, the world itself is upheld.

5.113Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

मां वा ममानुजं वापि निशुम्भमुरुविक्रमम्।
भज त्वं चञ्चलापाङ्गि रत्नभूतासि वै यतः ॥ ११३ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

माम् | वा | मम | अनुजम् | वा | अपि | निशुम्भम् | उरुविक्रमम्भज | त्वम् | चञ्चलापाङ्गि | रत्नभूता | असि | वै | यतः

3IAST

As recitedmāṃ vā mamānujaṃ vāpi niśumbhamuruvikramam |
bhaja tvaṃ cañcalāpāṅgi ratnabhūtāsi vai yataḥ ||

Separatedmām | vā | mama | anujam | vā | api | niśumbham | uruvikramam
bhaja | tvam | cañcalāpāṅgi | ratnabhūtā | asi | vai | yataḥ

4Word by word

माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of भज
वाvāeither, orindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
ममmamamyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with अनुजम्
अनुजम्anujamyounger brotherm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of भज
वाvāorindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
अपिapialso, evenindeclinable (particle); वा अपि ‘or else’
निशुम्भम्niśumbhamNiśumbham. acc. sg.; proper name; in apposition to अनुजम्
उरुविक्रमम्uruvikramamof wide-ranging valourm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies निशुम्भम्
भजbhajachoose, take (as husband); lit. ‘resort to, share in’√भज् (bhvādi 1U ‘to share, resort to’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
चञ्चलापाङ्गिcañcalāpāṅgiO you of the darting glancesf. voc. sg. of चञ्चलापाङ्गी; compound (see Samāsa)
रत्नभूताratnabhūtābeing a jewelf. nom. sg.; compound (रत्न + भूत, as in 5.111); predicate
असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi 2P ‘to be’), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
वैvaiindeedindeclinable (emphatic particle)
यतःyataḥsince, becauseindeclinable (causal conjunction)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • ममानुजं mamānujaṃ → मम + अनुजम्
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā; the final m becomes anusvāra before वा (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).
  • वापि vāpi → वा + अपि
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā; likewise रत्नभूतासि (रत्नभूता + असि).
  • निशुम्भमुरुविक्रमम् niśumbhamuruvikramam → निशुम्भम् + उरुविक्रमम्
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    The m is written together with the following vowel.
  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi → चञ्चल + अपाङ्गि
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अनुजम् anujam — upapada tatpuruṣa (अनु + √जन् + ḍa)
    Members: अनु + ज · Vigraha: अनु (पश्चात्) जातः, तम् anu (paścāt) jātaḥ, tam
    Literally “born after”; in the verse: younger brother
  • उरुविक्रमम् uruvikramam — bahuvrīhi
    Members: उरु + विक्रम · Vigraha: उरुः विक्रमः यस्य सः, तम् uruḥ vikramaḥ yasya saḥ, tam
    Literally “whose valour is wide”; in the verse: of great prowess
  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi — bahuvrīhi (feminine in -ī)
    Members: चञ्चल + अपाङ्ग · Vigraha: चञ्चलौ अपाङ्गौ यस्याः सा, तत्सम्बुद्धौ cañcalau apāṅgau yasyāḥ sā, tatsambuddhau
    Literally “she whose outer eye-corners are restless”; in the verse: a woman with lively, darting glances — a conventional epithet of beauty

C. Morphology

  • भज bhaja — Imperative of √भज्. Its basic sense is ‘to share, resort to’; with a man as object, of a woman, it means to choose him, give oneself to him in marriage.
  • चञ्चलापाङ्गि cañcalāpāṅgi — A bahuvrīhi ending in a body-part (अङ्ग) forms its feminine with -ī: चञ्चलापाङ्गी; the vocative shortens the ī: चञ्चलापाङ्गि.

D. Anvaya — prose order

चञ्चलापाङ्गि, यतः त्वं वै रत्नभूता असि, (अतः) मां वा मम अनुजम् उरुविक्रमं निशुम्भं वा अपि भज।

cañcalāpāṅgi, yataḥ tvaṃ vai ratnabhūtā asi, (ataḥ) māṃ vā mama anujam uruvikramaṃ niśumbhaṃ vā api bhaja |

An imperative (भज) with two alternative objects joined by वा … वा अपि, and a causal clause (यतः …) at the end.

6Translation

LiteralChoose me, or else my younger brother Niśumbha of wide valour, O you of darting glances — since you are indeed a jewel.

ReadableTake me, or my younger brother Niśumbha of mighty valour, O lady of the darting glance — for a jewel is what you are.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe proposal itself: the Devī may choose either brother. The reason is the same as in 5.112 — she is a jewel, and jewels go to the brothers.

Traditional interpretationThe offer of a choice between two husbands prepares the Devī’s answer: she will lay down one condition for either of them (5.120–121). The narrative leaves both brothers in the frame; both will meet her, but only in battle (chapters 9–10).

5.114Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

परमैश्वर्यमतुलं प्राप्स्यसे मत्परिग्रहात्।
एतद् बुद्ध्या समालोच्य मत्परिग्रहतां व्रज ॥ ११४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

परमैश्वर्यम् | अतुलम् | प्राप्स्यसे | मत्परिग्रहात्एतत् | बुद्ध्या | समालोच्य | मत्परिग्रहताम् | व्रज

3IAST

As recitedparamaiśvaryamatulaṃ prāpsyase matparigrahāt |
etad buddhyā samālocya matparigrahatāṃ vraja ||

Separatedparamaiśvaryam | atulam | prāpsyase | matparigrahāt
etat | buddhyā | samālocya | matparigrahatām | vraja

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
परमैश्वर्यम्paramaiśvaryamsupreme sovereignty, highest wealth and powern. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of प्राप्स्यसे
अतुलम्atulammatchlessn. acc. sg. of अतुल; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies परमैश्वर्यम्
प्राप्स्यसेprāpsyaseyou will obtainप्र + √आप् (svādi 5P ‘to reach, obtain’), lṛṭ (simple future), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, ātmanepada (classical प्राप्स्यसि)
मत्परिग्रहात्matparigrahātfrom being taken by me (as wife)m. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of cause
एतत्etatthispronoun एतद्, n. acc. sg.; object of समालोच्य
बुद्ध्याbuddhyāwith (your) understandingf. instr. sg. of बुद्धि (i-stem)
समालोच्यsamālocyahaving considered wellabsolutive (lyap) of सम् + आ + √लोच् (curādi 10 ‘to see, consider’)
मत्परिग्रहताम्matparigrahatāmthe state of being my consortf. acc. sg. of मत्परिग्रहता (compound + abstract suffix tal); goal of व्रज
व्रजvrajago to, enter (that state)√व्रज् (bhvādi 1P ‘to go’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • परमैश्वर्यमतुलं paramaiśvaryamatulaṃ → परमैश्वर्यम् + अतुलम्
    no change before a vowel; anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m joined to the vowel of अतुलम्; the m of अतुलम् becomes anusvāra before प्राप्स्यसे.
  • परमैश्वर्य paramaiśvarya → परम + ऐश्वर्य
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    a + ai → ai.
  • एतद् बुद्ध्या etad buddhyā → एतत् + बुद्ध्या
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before the voiced b. The Gita Press writes the d with a virāma and a space.
  • मत्परिग्रहात् matparigrahāt → मद् + परिग्रहात्
    consonant sandhi inside a compound (devoicing) · खरि च (8.4.55)
    The pronoun stem मद् becomes मत् before the unvoiced p.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • परमैश्वर्यम् paramaiśvaryam — karmadhāraya
    Members: परम + ऐश्वर्य · Vigraha: परमम् ऐश्वर्यम् paramam aiśvaryam
    Literally “supreme lordship”; in the verse: the highest wealth and power there is
  • अतुलम् atulam — nañ bahuvrīhi
    Members: अ (नञ्) + तुला · Vigraha: न विद्यते तुला यस्य तत् na vidyate tulā yasya tat
    Literally “having no balance (to be weighed against)”; in the verse: beyond comparison
  • मत्परिग्रहात् matparigrahāt — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (the genitive is subjective: ‘my taking (you)’)
    Members: मद् + परिग्रह · Vigraha: मम परिग्रहः, तस्मात् mama parigrahaḥ, tasmāt
    Literally “from my taking”; in the verse: through becoming my wife
  • मत्परिग्रहताम् matparigrahatām — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa with the abstract suffix -ता
    Members: मद् + परिग्रह (+ tal) · Vigraha: मम परिग्रहः मत्परिग्रहः; तस्य भावः मत्परिग्रहता, ताम् mama parigrahaḥ matparigrahaḥ; tasya bhāvaḥ matparigrahatā, tām
    Literally “the state of being my possession, my wife”; in the verse: become my consort
    Alternative: परिग्रह means both ‘taking, acceptance (in marriage)’ and ‘what is taken: wife, possession’. In मत्परिग्रहात् the act is meant, in मत्परिग्रहताम् the status; the same word was used of possessions in 5.98 (भ्रातुस्तव परिग्रहे).

C. Morphology

  • प्राप्स्यसे prāpsyase — Future of प्र + √आप् (आप् + स्य → आप्स्य-), with the ātmanepada ending -से where classical usage has the parasmaipada प्राप्स्यसि. Such voice changes are frequent in the epic and Purāṇic language.
  • मत्परिग्रहताम् matparigrahatām — The abstract suffix -ता (tal) makes a feminine noun of state from the compound: ‘my-consort-ness’. With a verb of motion (व्रज) the accusative names the condition one passes into: ‘go to the state of …’, i.e. ‘become …’.
  • समालोच्य samālocya — Absolutive of the tenth-class root √लोच् with two prefixes: the causative -ay- is dropped before -य (lyap). ‘Having weighed (it) well’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

मत्परिग्रहात् (त्वम्) अतुलं परमैश्वर्यं प्राप्स्यसे। एतत् बुद्ध्या समालोच्य मत्परिग्रहतां व्रज।

matparigrahāt (tvam) atulaṃ paramaiśvaryaṃ prāpsyase | etat buddhyā samālocya matparigrahatāṃ vraja |

A promise in the future (प्राप्स्यसे), then an imperative (व्रज) preceded by an absolutive (समालोच्य) with its object एतत्.

6Translation

LiteralYou will obtain supreme, matchless sovereignty from being taken by me. Having considered this well with your understanding, go to the state of being my consort.

ReadableAs my wife you will gain a sovereignty beyond compare. Weigh this well in your mind, and become my consort.”

The closing quotation mark ends the message quoted in Śumbha’s words (from 5.108).

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe message ends with an inducement and a command: marriage to Śumbha will give the Devī matchless power; she should think it over and consent.

Traditional interpretationThe offer reverses the truth the text has already stated: the gods’ hymn has just praised her as the power present in all beings (5.14–80), and the narrator will call her the one by whom the world is upheld (5.116). Śumbha offers her what is already hers.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the appeal to ‘consider it with your intelligence’ invites calculation. The Devī will answer not with a calculation but with a vow made long before (5.119–120).

5.116Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

इत्युक्ता सा तदा देवी गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता जगौ।
दुर्गा भगवती भद्रा ययेदं धार्यते जगत् ॥ ११६ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

इति | उक्ता | सा | तदा | देवी | गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता | जगौदुर्गा | भगवती | भद्रा | यया | इदम् | धार्यते | जगत्

3IAST

As recitedityuktā sā tadā devī gambhīrāntaḥsmitā jagau |
durgā bhagavatī bhadrā yayedaṃ dhāryate jagat ||

Separatediti | uktā | sā | tadā | devī | gambhīrāntaḥsmitā | jagau
durgā | bhagavatī | bhadrā | yayā | idam | dhāryate | jagat

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
इतिitithusindeclinable (quotative particle: closes the reported speech)
उक्ताuktāaddressed, spoken topast passive participle (kta) of √वच्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies सा देवी
साsāshe, thatpronoun तद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies देवी
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
देवीdevīthe Goddessf. nom. sg. of देवी; subject of जगौ
गम्भीरान्तःस्मिताgambhīrāntaḥsmitāgrave, and smiling withinf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies देवी
जगौjagauspoke; lit. ‘sang’√गै (bhvādi 1P ‘to sing’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
दुर्गाdurgāDurgāf. nom. sg. of दुर्गा; in apposition to देवी
भगवतीbhagavatīthe Blessed Onef. nom. sg. of भगवती (भगवत् + ṅīp); in apposition to देवी
भद्राbhadrāthe auspiciousf. nom. sg. of भद्रा; in apposition to देवी
ययाyayāby whomrelative pronoun यद्, f. instr. sg.; agent of धार्यते
इदम्idamthispronoun इदम्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies जगत्
धार्यतेdhāryateis upheld, is sustained√धृ (bhvādi 1U ‘to hold’), causative stem धारय-, karmaṇi prayoga, laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
जगत्jagatworldn. nom. sg. of जगत्; subject of the passive

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • इत्युक्ता ityuktā → इति + उक्ता
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i before u becomes y.
  • गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता gambhīrāntaḥsmitā → गम्भीर + अन्तः + स्मिता
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha); visarga kept · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā. The visarga of अन्तः stays before the sibilant s inside the compound.
  • ययेदं yayedaṃ → यया + इदम्
    vowel sandhi (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    ā + i → e; the final m of इदम् becomes anusvāra before धार्यते (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता gambhīrāntaḥsmitā — karmadhāraya (two attributes of one subject); inside it अन्तःस्मिता = अन्तः स्मिता ‘smiling within’ (the adverb अन्तः with the participle स्मिता)
    Members: गम्भीरा + अन्तःस्मिता · Vigraha: गम्भीरा च सा अन्तःस्मिता च gambhīrā ca sā antaḥsmitā ca
    Literally “grave and inwardly smiling”; in the verse: outwardly serious, with a smile kept inside
    Alternative: Also read as a bahuvrīhi, गम्भीरम् अन्तः स्मितं यस्याः सा ‘she whose inner smile was deep’. Both keep the contrast of a grave face and a hidden smile; the karmadhāraya keeps the simpler parts.

C. Morphology

  • जगौ jagau — Perfect of √गै ‘sing’: reduplicated ज- (g → j) + गौ. Used here, as often in the Purāṇas, simply for ‘spoke, declared’.
  • धार्यते dhāryate — Passive of the causative धारय- (√धृ ‘to hold’): the causative -ay- is dropped before the passive -ya-: धार्-य-ते, ‘is upheld’.
  • उक्ता uktā — Past participle of √वच् with samprasāraṇa (va → u): उक्त. In the passive sense of a verb of speaking it means ‘the one spoken to’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

इति उक्ता सा देवी, दुर्गा भगवती भद्रा, यया इदं जगत् धार्यते, तदा गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता जगौ।

iti uktā sā devī, durgā bhagavatī bhadrā, yayā idaṃ jagat dhāryate, tadā gambhīrāntaḥsmitā jagau |

The subject देवी has three appositive names and a relative clause (यया … धार्यते); the main verb is जगौ with the predicate adjective गम्भीरान्तःस्मिता.

6Translation

LiteralThus addressed, the Devī then, grave and smiling within, spoke — Durgā, the Blessed, the Auspicious, by whom this world is upheld.

ReadableSo addressed, the Devī — Durgā, the Blessed, the Auspicious, by whom this world is upheld — grave of face yet smiling inwardly, gave her answer.

The appositive names are brought forward next to देवी.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe narrator introduces the Devī’s reply. The contrast is marked: she is grave, but smiles within. The second line steps out of the scene to remind the hearer who is being asked to marry Śumbha — the one by whom the world is upheld.

Traditional interpretationThe hidden smile is read as the key to her reply: what she says will be courteous and literally true, while its meaning is the opposite of surrender. The name Durgā, ‘hard to approach’, is apt at the very moment an asura proposes to take her.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāLalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has two of the names given here: 190, दुर्गा, and 279, भगवती. The coincidence of names reflects a shared Śākta vocabulary; the two texts are separate works.

5.118Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

सत्यमुक्तं त्वया नात्र मिथ्या किञ्चित्त्वयोदितम्।
त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः ॥ ११८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

सत्यम् | उक्तम् | त्वया | न | अत्र | मिथ्या | किञ्चित् | त्वया | उदितम्त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः | शुम्भः | निशुम्भः | च | अपि | तादृशः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • नात्र nātra → न + अत्र
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.
  • किञ्चित्त्वयोदितम् kiñcittvayoditam → किञ्चित् + त्वया + उदितम्
    no change; vowel sandhi (guṇa) · खरि च (8.4.55); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    The t of किञ्चित् stays t before the t of त्वया (written as a double t); then ā + u → o.
  • शुम्भो निशुम्भश्चापि śumbho niśumbhaścāpi → शुम्भः + निशुम्भः + च + अपि
    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    -aḥ before the voiced n becomes -o; visarga before c becomes ś; a + a → ā.
  • त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः trailokyādhipatiḥ → त्रैलोक्य + अधिपतिः
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः trailokyādhipatiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: त्रैलोक्य + अधिपति · Vigraha: त्रैलोक्यस्य अधिपतिः trailokyasya adhipatiḥ
    Literally “overlord of the three worlds”; in the verse: ruler of the whole universe

C. Morphology

  • उदितम् uditam — Past participle of √वद् with samprasāraṇa (va → u) and the connecting i: उद्-इत. Synonymous with उक्तम् in the first half; the line says the same thing twice, positively and negatively.
  • तादृशः tādṛśaḥ — तद् + दृश ‘looking like that’: the pronoun stem lengthens its vowel (ता-) before दृश. ‘Such’, i.e. of the same rank as Śumbha.

D. Anvaya — prose order

त्वया सत्यम् उक्तम्; अत्र त्वया किञ्चित् मिथ्या न उदितम्। शुम्भः त्रैलोक्याधिपतिः, निशुम्भः च अपि तादृशः (अस्ति)।

tvayā satyam uktam; atra tvayā kiñcit mithyā na uditam | śumbhaḥ trailokyādhipatiḥ, niśumbhaḥ ca api tādṛśaḥ (asti) |

Two passive statements with the participles उक्तम् and उदितम् (verb ‘is’ understood), then two nominal sentences.

6Translation

LiteralTruth has been spoken by you; nothing false at all has been uttered by you here. Śumbha is the overlord of the three worlds, and Niśumbha too is such.

ReadableWhat you say is true; you have spoken no falsehood here. Śumbha is lord of the three worlds, and Niśumbha is his equal.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Devī begins by granting everything the messenger has said about the brothers’ power. She does not dispute their rank; her objection will lie elsewhere.

Traditional interpretationRead with the smile of 5.116, the concession is ironic: she accepts the facts in order to bring in a condition the facts cannot meet. Note that 5.128, as the Gita Press prints it, does not repeat the phrase निशुम्भश्चापि तादृशः, though other witnesses read it there too (see 5.128).

5.119Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

किं त्वत्र यत्प्रतिज्ञातं मिथ्या तत्क्रियते कथम्।
श्रूयतामल्पबुद्धित्वात्प्रतिज्ञा या कृता पुरा ॥ ११९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

किम् | तु | अत्र | यत् | प्रतिज्ञातम् | मिथ्या | तत् | क्रियते | कथम्श्रूयताम् | अल्पबुद्धित्वात् | प्रतिज्ञा | या | कृता | पुरा

3IAST

As recitedkiṃ tvatra yatpratijñātaṃ mithyā tatkriyate katham |
śrūyatāmalpabuddhitvātpratijñā yā kṛtā purā ||

Separatedkim | tu | atra | yat | pratijñātam | mithyā | tat | kriyate | katham
śrūyatām | alpabuddhitvāt | pratijñā | yā | kṛtā | purā

4Word by word

किम्kimbut (with तु)indeclinable (किम्; with तु forms the adversative ‘but’)
तुtubutindeclinable (adversative particle)
अत्रatrahere, in this matterindeclinable (adverb of place)
यत्yatwhatrelative pronoun यद्, n. nom. sg.; correlative तत्
प्रतिज्ञातम्pratijñātamhas been promised, was vowedpast passive participle (kta) of प्रति + √ज्ञा (kryādi 9 ‘to know’; with प्रति ‘to promise’), n. nom. sg.

श्रूयताम्śrūyatāmlet it be heard, hear√श्रु (svādi 5 ‘to hear’), karmaṇi prayoga, loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana

प्रतिज्ञाpratijñāthe vow, the promisef. nom. sg. of प्रतिज्ञा; subject of the passive श्रूयताम्
याyāwhichrelative pronoun यद्, f. nom. sg.; qualifies प्रतिज्ञा
कृताkṛtāwas madepast passive participle (kta) of √कृ, f. nom. sg.
पुराpurāformerly, long agoindeclinable (adverb of time)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • किं त्वत्र kiṃ tvatra → किम् + तु + अत्र
    anusvāra sandhi; vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    Final m before t becomes anusvāra; u before a becomes v.
  • यत्प्रतिज्ञातं yatpratijñātaṃ → यत् + प्रतिज्ञातम्
    no change · खरि च (8.4.55)
    Final t stays unvoiced before the unvoiced p; likewise तत्क्रियते and अल्पबुद्धित्वात्प्रतिज्ञा.
  • श्रूयतामल्पबुद्धित्वात् śrūyatāmalpabuddhitvāt → श्रूयताम् + अल्पबुद्धित्वात्
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    The m is written together with the following vowel.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अल्पबुद्धित्वात् alpabuddhitvāt — bahuvrīhi (अल्पबुद्धि) with the abstract suffix -त्व
    Members: अल्प + बुद्धि (+ tva) · Vigraha: अल्पा बुद्धिः यस्याः सा अल्पबुद्धिः; तस्याः भावः अल्पबुद्धित्वम्, तस्मात् alpā buddhiḥ yasyāḥ sā alpabuddhiḥ; tasyāḥ bhāvaḥ alpabuddhitvam, tasmāt
    Literally “from the state of being one whose understanding is small”; in the verse: out of thoughtlessness, foolishly

C. Morphology

  • क्रियते kriyate — Passive present of √कृ: the root becomes क्रि- before the passive -य- (क्रियते). With मिथ्या: ‘is made false’; with कथम्, ‘how can it be made false?’.
  • श्रूयताम् śrūyatām — Passive imperative of √श्रु (the root vowel lengthened before -य-: श्रूय-): ‘let it be heard’, the polite form of ‘hear’.
  • अल्पबुद्धित्वात् alpabuddhitvāt — Ablative of cause with the abstract -त्व: ‘because of being of small understanding’. Feigned modesty: compare अनालोचिता ‘unconsidered’ in 5.128.

D. Anvaya — prose order

किं तु अत्र यत् प्रतिज्ञातं, तत् कथं मिथ्या क्रियते? अल्पबुद्धित्वात् (मया) पुरा या प्रतिज्ञा कृता, (सा) श्रूयताम्।

kiṃ tu atra yat pratijñātaṃ, tat kathaṃ mithyā kriyate? alpabuddhitvāt (mayā) purā yā pratijñā kṛtā, (sā) śrūyatām |

A rhetorical question built on a relative pair (यत् … तत्), then a passive imperative whose subject प्रतिज्ञा has a relative clause (या कृता पुरा).

6Translation

LiteralBut here — what has been vowed, how is that to be made false? Let the vow be heard which was made formerly out of little understanding.

ReadableBut in this matter there is a vow I made, and how could I break it? Hear the promise I once made, foolishly.

The passive constructions are turned active (‘I made’, ‘could I break’); the agent ‘I’ is implied by the context.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Devī introduces an obstacle: a vow she made long ago. She cannot make it false, and she calls it the product of her ‘little understanding’.

Traditional interpretationThe self-deprecation is read as irony matching her inward smile (5.116): the Goddess who is praised as बुद्धि itself (5.20) claims to have acted from small understanding. The vow is not a mistake but the trap into which the asuras will walk.

5.120Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

यो मां जयति संग्रामे यो मे दर्पं व्यपोहति।
यो मे प्रतिबलो लोके स मे भर्ता भविष्यति ॥ १२० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

यः | माम् | जयति | संग्रामे | यः | मे | दर्पम् | व्यपोहतियः | मे | प्रतिबलः | लोके | सः | मे | भर्ता | भविष्यति

3IAST

As recitedyo māṃ jayati saṃgrāme yo me darpaṃ vyapohati |
yo me pratibalo loke sa me bhartā bhaviṣyati ||

Separatedyaḥ | mām | jayati | saṃgrāme | yaḥ | me | darpam | vyapohati
yaḥ | me | pratibalaḥ | loke | saḥ | me | bhartā | bhaviṣyati

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of जयति
माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of जयति
जयतिjayaticonquers, defeats√जि (bhvādi 1P ‘to conquer’), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
संग्रामेsaṃgrāmein battlem. loc. sg. of संग्राम
यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject of व्यपोहति
मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with दर्पम्
दर्पम्darpampridem. acc. sg. of दर्प; object of व्यपोहति
व्यपोहतिvyapohatiremoves, drives awayवि + अप + √ऊह् (bhvādi 1 ‘to push, remove’; here parasmaipada), laṭ (present), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana
यःyaḥhe whorelative pronoun यद्, m. nom. sg.; subject (verb ‘is’ understood)
मेmeto me, myfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with प्रतिबलः ‘a match for me’
प्रतिबलःpratibalaḥequal in strength, a matchm. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); predicate
लोकेlokein the worldm. loc. sg. of लोक
सःsaḥhepronoun तद्, m. nom. sg.; correlative of the three यः; subject of भविष्यति
मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with भर्ता
भर्ताbhartāhusbandm. nom. sg. of भर्तृ (√भृ + tṛc, ‘supporter’); predicate of सः
भविष्यतिbhaviṣyatiwill be√भू (bhvādi 1P), lṛṭ (simple future), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • यो मां yo māṃ → यः + माम्
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before a voiced consonant becomes -o; the same in यो मे (twice) and प्रतिबलो लोके.
  • दर्पं व्यपोहति darpaṃ vyapohati → दर्पम् + व्यपोहति
    anusvāra sandhi · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m before a consonant is written as anusvāra.
  • व्यपोहति vyapohati → वि + अप + ऊहति
    vowel sandhi between prefixes and root (yaṇ, guṇa) · इको यणचि (6.1.77); आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    i before a becomes y (व्यप-); a + ū → o (अपोह-).
  • स मे sa me → सः + मे
    visarga sandhi (special rule for सः) · एतत्तदोः सुलोपोऽकोरनञ्समासे हलि (6.1.132)
    The visarga of सः is dropped before a consonant.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • प्रतिबलः pratibalaḥ — bahuvrīhi (prādi-type, with the prefix प्रति ‘matching’)
    Members: प्रति + बल · Vigraha: प्रति (समानं) बलं यस्य सः prati (samānaṃ) balaṃ yasya saḥ
    Literally “one whose strength is counter (to mine)”; in the verse: one equal to me in strength
    Alternative: Also read as a prādi tatpuruṣa, प्रतिगतं बलम् ‘a counter-force’, used of a person; the bahuvrīhi suits the masculine agreement.

C. Morphology

  • जयति jayati — Present tense in a general relative clause with future sense: ‘whoever conquers (= shall conquer) me’. The main clause has the future भविष्यति.
  • भर्ता bhartā — Nominative singular of the agent noun भर्तृ (√भृ ‘to bear, support’ + -तृ): literally ‘supporter, maintainer’, the ordinary word for ‘husband’.
  • व्यपोहति vyapohati — वि + अप + √ऊह् ‘push away, dispel’. The root is listed as ātmanepada; the parasmaipada व्यपोहति is the usual epic form.

D. Anvaya — prose order

यः मां संग्रामे जयति, यः मे दर्पं व्यपोहति, यः लोके मे प्रतिबलः (अस्ति), सः मे भर्ता भविष्यति।

yaḥ māṃ saṃgrāme jayati, yaḥ me darpaṃ vyapohati, yaḥ loke me pratibalaḥ (asti), saḥ me bhartā bhaviṣyati |

Three relative clauses, each opened by यः, all taken up by the single correlative सः of the main clause.

6Translation

LiteralHe who conquers me in battle, he who removes my pride, he who is my match in strength in the world — he will be my husband.

Readable“He who conquers me in battle, who humbles my pride, who is my equal in strength in all the world — he shall be my husband.”

Set in quotation marks as the words of the vow announced in 5.119.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe vow itself, in three conditions that come to one: the husband must defeat her in battle, break her pride, and match her strength. The repetition of यः … यः … यः gives it the form of a formal pledge.

Traditional interpretationSince the text has already shown her to be the power of all the gods together (2.9–30) and the power present in all beings (5.14–80), no one can meet the condition. The tradition reads the vow as a statement of her sovereignty: the Goddess is not won as a possession, and her only ‘husband’ would be her equal.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the Devī answers a claim of ownership with a test of strength. She does not argue with Śumbha’s logic of jewels; she changes the terms on which it can be applied.

5.121Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तदागच्छतु शुम्भोऽत्र निशुम्भो वा महासुरः।
मां जित्वा किं चिरेणात्र पाणिं गृह्णातु मे लघु ॥ १२१ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तत् | आगच्छतु | शुम्भः | अत्र | निशुम्भः | वा | महासुरःमाम् | जित्वा | किम् | चिरेण | अत्र | पाणिम् | गृह्णातु | मे | लघु

3IAST

As recitedtadāgacchatu śumbho'tra niśumbho vā mahāsuraḥ |
māṃ jitvā kiṃ cireṇātra pāṇiṃ gṛhṇātu me laghu ||

Separatedtat | āgacchatu | śumbhaḥ | atra | niśumbhaḥ | vā | mahāsuraḥ
mām | jitvā | kim | cireṇa | atra | pāṇim | gṛhṇātu | me | laghu

4Word by word

तत्tatso, thereforepronoun तद्, n. acc. sg., used as an indeclinable (‘that being so’)
आगच्छतुāgacchatulet (him) comeआ + √गम् (bhvādi 1P, stem गच्छ-), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
शुम्भःśumbhaḥŚumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; subject
अत्रatrahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
निशुम्भःniśumbhaḥNiśumbham. nom. sg.; proper name; alternative subject
वाvāorindeclinable (disjunctive particle)
महासुरःmahāsuraḥthe great asuram. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies निशुम्भः
माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of जित्वा
जित्वाjitvāhaving conqueredabsolutive (ktvā) of √जि (bhvādi 1P ‘to conquer’)
किम्kimwhat (use)?, why?interrogative pronoun किम्, n. nom. sg., used adverbially

अत्रatrahereindeclinable (adverb of place)
पाणिम्pāṇimhandm. acc. sg. of पाणि (i-stem); object of गृह्णातु
गृह्णातुgṛhṇātulet (him) take√ग्रह् (kryādi 9P ‘to seize’, stem गृह्णा-), loṭ (imperative), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.; with पाणिम्
लघुlaghuquicklyn. acc. sg. of लघु, used adverbially

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तदागच्छतु tadāgacchatu → तत् + आगच्छतु
    consonant sandhi (voicing) · झलां जशोऽन्ते (8.2.39)
    Final t becomes d before a vowel.
  • शुम्भोऽत्र śumbho'tra → शुम्भः + अत्र
    visarga sandhi with pūrvarūpa · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), अतो रोरप्लुतादप्लुते (6.1.113), आद्गुणः (6.1.87), एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)
    -aḥ before a becomes -o, and the following a is absorbed (avagraha).
  • निशुम्भो वा niśumbho vā → निशुम्भः + वा
    visarga sandhi · हशि च (6.1.114), आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    -aḥ before the voiced v becomes -o.
  • चिरेणात्र cireṇātra → चिरेण + अत्र
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • महासुरः mahāsuraḥ — karmadhāraya
    Members: महत् + असुर · Vigraha: महान् चासौ असुरः च mahān cāsau asuraḥ ca
    Literally “a great asura”; in the verse: the mighty demon (महत् becomes महा- before a noun in the same case)

C. Morphology

  • गृह्णातु gṛhṇātu — Imperative of √ग्रह्, ninth class, with samprasāraṇa (ग्रह् → गृह्) before -णा-: गृह्णातु. पाणिं गृह्णातु ‘let him take (my) hand’ is the idiom for marriage (पाणिग्रहण).
  • जित्वा jitvā — Absolutive of √जि without a prefix: -त्वा. It sets the condition before the marriage: first conquer, then take the hand.
  • किम् kim — With the instrumental, किम् asks ‘what (is gained) by …?’: किं चिरेण ‘why delay?’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तत् शुम्भः महासुरः निशुम्भः वा अत्र आगच्छतु। मां जित्वा मे पाणिम् अत्र लघु गृह्णातु। चिरेण किम्?

tat śumbhaḥ mahāsuraḥ niśumbhaḥ vā atra āgacchatu | māṃ jitvā me pāṇim atra laghu gṛhṇātu | cireṇa kim?

Two third-person imperatives (आगच्छतु, गृह्णातु), the second preceded by the absolutive जित्वा; the rhetorical किं चिरेण stands between them.

6Translation

LiteralSo let Śumbha come here, or Niśumbha the great asura; having conquered me — what (use) is delay? — let him take my hand here quickly.

ReadableSo let Śumbha come here, or Niśumbha, the mighty asura. Let him conquer me and, without delay, take my hand.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe Devī applies the vow to the proposal. Either brother may come, as Śumbha offered (5.113), but first he must defeat her; then let him marry her at once.

Traditional interpretationThe urgency (किं चिरेण, लघु) mirrors the messenger’s demand that she come quickly (5.103, लघु). The invitation is a challenge: the next chapters bring first the asura generals and finally the two brothers to fight her.

5.122Uvāca · speaker

1Original śloka

दूत उवाच ॥ १२२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

दूतः | उवाच

3IAST

As reciteddūta uvāca ||

Separateddūtaḥ | uvāca

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
दूतःdūtaḥthe messenger (Sugrīva)m. nom. sg. of दूत; subject of उवाच
उवाचuvācasaid√वच् (adādi 2P ‘to speak’), liṭ (perfect), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • दूत उवाच dūta uvāca → दूतः + उवाच
    visarga sandhi · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), भोभगोअघोअपूर्वस्य योऽशि (8.3.17), लोपः शाकल्यस्य (8.3.19)
    -aḥ before a vowel other than a: the visarga becomes y, the y is dropped, and the vowels stand apart (as in 1.1).

C. Morphology

  • उवाच uvāca — Perfect of √वच्, as in 1.1.

D. Anvaya — prose order

दूतः उवाच।

dūtaḥ uvāca |

A speaker label.

6Translation

LiteralThe messenger said:

ReadableThe messenger said:

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningSugrīva, Śumbha’s envoy, answers the Devī’s vow (5.120–121) with scorn and threats; his speech runs to 5.126.

5.123Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

अवलिप्तासि मैवं त्वं देवि ब्रूहि ममाग्रतः।
त्रैलोक्ये कः पुमांस्तिष्ठेदग्रे शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः ॥ १२३ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

अवलिप्ता | असि | मा | एवम् | त्वम् | देवि | ब्रूहि | मम | अग्रतःत्रैलोक्ये | कः | पुमान् | तिष्ठेत् | अग्रे | शुम्भनिशुम्भयोः

3IAST

As recitedavaliptāsi maivaṃ tvaṃ devi brūhi mamāgrataḥ |
trailokye kaḥ pumāṃstiṣṭhedagre śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ ||

Separatedavaliptā | asi | mā | evam | tvam | devi | brūhi | mama | agrataḥ
trailokye | kaḥ | pumān | tiṣṭhet | agre | śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
अवलिप्ताavaliptāarrogant; lit. ‘smeared (with pride)’past passive participle (kta) of अव + √लिप् (tudādi 6U ‘to smear’), f. nom. sg.; predicate
असिasiyou are√अस् (adādi 2P ‘to be’), laṭ (present), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
माmādo notindeclinable (prohibitive particle)
एवम्evamthus, like thisindeclinable (adverb of manner)
त्वम्tvamyousecond-person pronoun युष्मद्, nom. sg.; subject of ब्रूहि
देविdeviO Devī, O ladyf. voc. sg. of देवी
ब्रूहिbrūhispeak√ब्रू (adādi 2U ‘to speak’), loṭ (imperative), madhyama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
ममmamaof mefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, gen. sg.; with अग्रतः
अग्रतःagrataḥin front of, in the presence ofindeclinable (अग्र + tasil), governing the genitive मम
त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य
